### [1] [Tortho-Gaussian: Splatting True Digital Orthophoto Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19594)
*Xin Wang,Wendi Zhang,Hong Xie,Haibin Ai,Qiangqiang Yuan,Zongqian Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TOrtho-Gaussian方法，基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）通过正交投影高斯核生成真正射影像（TDOM），避免了传统方法中数字表面模型（DSM）和遮挡检测的需求，采用分治策略处理大场景，并设计了全各向异性高斯核以提升反射面和细长结构的渲染质量。实验表明该方法在建筑边界精度、低纹理区域和建筑立面视觉质量上优于现有商业软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TDOM生成流程复杂，依赖精确的DSM、遮挡检测等步骤，容易因DSM不准确、遮挡检测退化、弱纹理区域及反射表面视觉伪影等问题导致质量下降。作者希望提出一种更简洁、鲁棒且可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 1) 基于3DGS，将优化的各向异性高斯核正交投影到2D图像平面，直接生成TDOM，无需显式DSM和遮挡检测；2) 采用分治策略处理大规模场景，优化训练和渲染的内存与时间效率；3) 设计全各向异性高斯核，自适应不同区域特征，改善反射表面和细长结构的渲染质量。

Result: 在建筑边界精度、低纹理区域和建筑立面视觉质量等方面，该方法优于现有商业软件。

Conclusion: TOrtho-Gaussian为大规模城市场景重建提供了一种鲁棒且可扩展的替代方案，有效提升了TDOM的质量和可扩展性。

Abstract: True Digital Orthophoto Maps (TDOMs) are essential products for digital twins and Geographic Information Systems (GIS). Traditionally, TDOM generation involves a complex set of traditional photogrammetric process, which may deteriorate due to various challenges, including inaccurate Digital Surface Model (DSM), degenerated occlusion detections, and visual artifacts in weak texture regions and reflective surfaces, etc. To address these challenges, we introduce TOrtho-Gaussian, a novel method inspired by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) that generates TDOMs through orthogonal splatting of optimized anisotropic Gaussian kernel. More specifically, we first simplify the orthophoto generation by orthographically splatting the Gaussian kernels onto 2D image planes, formulating a geometrically elegant solution that avoids the need for explicit DSM and occlusion detection. Second, to produce TDOM of large-scale area, a divide-and-conquer strategy is adopted to optimize memory usage and time efficiency of training and rendering for 3DGS. Lastly, we design a fully anisotropic Gaussian kernel that adapts to the varying characteristics of different regions, particularly improving the rendering quality of reflective surfaces and slender structures. Extensive experimental evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms existing commercial software in several aspects, including the accuracy of building boundaries, the visual quality of low-texture regions and building facades. These results underscore the potential of our approach for large-scale urban scene reconstruction, offering a robust alternative for enhancing TDOM quality and scalability.

</details>


### [2] [Not All Relations Rotate Alike: Transformation-Aware Decoupling for Viewpoint-Robust 3D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27412)
*Jingjun Sun,Chaowei Wang,Zhirui Liu,Jiaxu Tian,Ming Yang,Yaoxing Wang,Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出变换感知解耦（TAD）框架，通过将关系推理分解为视角不变分支和视角变换分支，解决了3D场景图生成中因偏航旋转导致的谓词预测不一致问题，在不使用旋转增强的情况下实现了SOTA的视角鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DSGG模型在偏航旋转下产生的谓词预测不符合预期的变换行为：方向性谓词（如左/前/右/后）应随观察帧变换，而接触/支撑/语义谓词应保持稳定。这一谓词级变换异质性导致了经验上的不匹配问题。

Method: 提出TAD框架：1) 将关系推理解耦为两个分支——稳定分支学习跨视角不变线索，方向分支学习随观察帧变化的线索；2) 融合两分支进行标准多标签谓词预测；3) 引入变换特定描述子和组感知辅助监督促进两分支捕捉互补关系线索。

Result: 在3DSSG数据集上，TAD在偏航视角变化下达到SOTA鲁棒性，且无需训练时旋转增强；同时在标准基准上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模谓词变换行为的异构性，TAD有效弥合了3DSGG在视角变换下的预测不一致问题，证明了谓词级解耦策略对视角鲁棒3D场景理解的有效性。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graph Generation (3DSGG) represents 3D scenes as structured object-relation-object graphs, providing a compact relational abstraction for spatial understanding. In embodied intelligence settings, the same 3D scene may be observed by agents from viewpoints that differ by yaw rotations. However, current 3DSGG models often fail to produce relation predictions that follow the expected transformation behavior under such viewpoint shifts. This behavior reveals an empirical mismatch related to predicate-level transformation heterogeneity: directional predicates such as left, front, right, and behind should transform with the observation frame, whereas most contact, support, and semantic predicates such as standing on and attached to should remain stable. To reduce this mismatch, we propose Transformation-Aware Decoupling (TAD), a viewpoint-robust 3DSGG framework that decouples relation reasoning according to predicate transformation behavior and is supported by viewpoint-stable object representations. TAD decomposes relation reasoning into two parts: one learns cues that should stay stable across viewpoints, while the other learns directional cues that should change with the observation frame. The two parts are merged for standard multi-label predicate prediction. Transformation-specific descriptors and group-aware auxiliary supervision encourage the two branches to capture complementary relation cues. Extensive experiments on 3DSSG show that TAD achieves state-of-the-art robustness under yaw viewpoint changes without training-time rotation augmentation, while maintaining competitive performance under the standard benchmark. The project page is available at https://tad-predicate.github.io/.

</details>


### [3] [STAG: Spatio-temporal Evolving Structural Representation of Action Units for Micro-expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28083)
*Nandani Sharma,Varun Sharma,Dinesh Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAG框架，通过动态ROI-AU耦合时空网络联合建模运动流和自适应面部连接，利用光流、双分支架构（图注意力+Transformer）、双向交叉注意力及AU引导的动态连接，实现跨数据集的微表情识别，在六个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微表情识别方法过度依赖顶点开始帧、忽视细粒度帧间动态、且分别建模时空信息，导致跨数据集泛化能力受限。

Method: 1) 基于幅度选择和时序注意力的光流提取；2) 双分支架构：增强图注意力网络用于结构化空间推理 + Transformer编码器用于时序建模；3) 双向交叉注意力模块实现时空特征互增强；4) AU引导的动态连接自适应调整面部区域交互；5) 焦损函数优化。

Result: 在CASME II、4DME、DFME、NaME、SAMM、SMIC-HS六个数据集上实验，展现出更强的鲁棒性、泛化性、可解释性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 自适应关系推理、AU引导的动态连接和深度时空特征融合能有效提升跨数据集微表情识别的准确性。

Abstract: Micro-expression recognition is challenging due to subtle and short-lived facial muscle movements. Existing methods rely heavily on apex-onset frames, overlook fine-grained inter-frame dynamics, and separately model spatial and temporal information, limiting generalization across datasets. To address these challenges, we propose STAG, a dynamic ROI-AU-coupled spatial-temporal network that jointly models motion flow and adaptive facial connectivity. The framework extracts optical flow from discriminative frames using magnitude-based selection and temporal attention. A dual-branch architecture combines an enhanced graph attention network for structured spatial reasoning with a transformer encoder for temporal modeling. A bidirectional cross-attention module enables mutual refinement of spatial and temporal features, while AU-guided dynamic connectivity adapts facial region interactions according to muscle activation patterns. The transformer captures subtle temporal dynamics beyond apex-based approaches, improving semantic consistency and interpretability for explainable micro-expression recognition. The fused representation is optimized using focal loss and evaluated on CASME II, 4DME, DFME, NaME, SAMM, and SMIC-HS. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved robustness, generalization, interpretability, and computational efficiency, confirming the effectiveness of adaptive relational reasoning, AU-guided dynamic connectivity, and deep spatial-temporal feature fusion for accurate cross-dataset micro-expression recognition.

</details>


### [4] [HAT-4D: Lifting Monocular Video for 4D Multi-Object Interactions via Human-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28215)
*Jiaxin Li,Yuxiang Wu,Zhenkai Zhang,Xinrui Shi,Haoyuan Wang,Yichen Zhao,Su Linxiang,Chenyang Yu,Mingyu Zhang,Yifan Ding,Boran Wen,Li Zhang,Ruiyang Liu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAT-4D是一个智能体框架，能从单目视频中重建多物体交互的4D信息（3D几何、时间动态和物理交互），利用VLM和多级人机反馈机制解决深度模糊和遮挡问题，并构建了MVOIK-4D基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目4D重建方法主要关注孤立物体，在多物体交互场景中因严重遮挡和复杂动态而表现不佳。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现从大规模野外单目视频中提取动态4D物体交互，为具身AI和VLA训练提供高效数据采集途径。

Method: 提出HAT-4D框架，集成视觉语言模型（VLM）与多级人机反馈机制，在3D生成和4D传播过程中解决深度模糊和交互遮挡问题；基于该框架构建MVOIK-4D开放世界基准数据集，并设计面向物理合理性和时间一致性的多维评估协议。

Result: HAT-4D在大多数评估指标上达到SOTA性能，同时保持有竞争力的语义对齐；消融实验表明少量人工反馈即可改善交互重建；框架生成的数据用于微调能有效提升基线模型性能。

Conclusion: HAT-4D作为可扩展的数据引擎，无需昂贵多相机设备即可生成物理合理的4D交互资产，为单目4D交互重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Extracting dynamic 4D object interactions from massive, in-the-wild monocular videos offers a highly efficient data collection pathway for scaling Embodied AI and training VLAs. However, existing monocular 4D reconstruction methods primarily focus on isolated objects, often failing under the severe occlusions and complex dynamics inherent in multi-object interactions. To bridge this gap, we propose HAT-4D, the first agentic framework designed to reconstruct the 3D geometry, temporal dynamics, and physical interactions of multiple objects from a single video. By integrating VLMs with a multi-level human-in-the-loop feedback mechanism, HAT-4D efficiently resolves depth ambiguities and interaction-induced occlusions during 3D generation and 4D propagation, yielding physically plausible assets without relying on expensive multicamera rigs. As a scalable data engine, HAT-4D facilitates the creation of MVOIK-4D, an open-world benchmark for monocular 4D interaction reconstruction, accompanied by a novel multi-dimensional evaluation protocol focused on physical plausibility and temporal consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HAT-4D achieves SOTA performance on most evaluation metrics, while maintaining competitive semantic alignment. Ablation studies show that introducing a small amount of human feedback improves interaction reconstruction. Moreover, the data produced by HAT-4D effectively improves baseline performance when used for fine-tuning. Our data and code are available at https://lijiaxin0111.github.io/HAT4D/

</details>


### [5] [SemCityLoc: Aerial 6DoF Localization Using Semantic 3D City Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27444)
*Jingfeng Mao,Xuyang Chen,Qilin Zhang,Oussema Dhaouadi,Guangming Wang,Brian Sheil,Daniel Cremers,Yan Xia,Olaf Wysocki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemCityLoc提出了一种基于语义几何对齐的空中6DoF定位方法，利用基础模型得到的视觉先验与标准化LoD城市模型进行结构化的语义表面对齐，无需GNSS或辐射度量场景重建。在自建基准SemCityLockeD上，该方法将定位召回率提升最高36%，平均位置误差从9.89m降至2.62m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空中6DoF定位依赖精确GNSS信号或辐射度量丰富的3D重建，限制了可扩展性和机载部署能力。作者希望在不依赖辐射度量场景重建的前提下，利用语义结构化几何约束实现高精度空中定位。

Method: 将空中姿态估计重新定义为结构化表面配准问题，对齐基础模型派生的视觉先验（语义表面与单目深度）与标准LoD（LoD1-LoD3）语义3D城市模型，替代稀疏轮廓或密集纹理匹配，增强重复与遮挡城市场景中的位姿可区分性。

Result: 在SemCityLockeD基准上，相比现有地图方法召回率提升最高36%，平均位置误差从9.89m降至2.62m，在挑战性城市峡谷场景中表现显著优势。

Conclusion: 语义结构化几何能为高精度空中定位提供充分且可扩展的约束，无需辐射度量场景重建。

Abstract: Aerial 6DoF localization typically relies on precise GNSS signals or radiometrically rich 3D reconstructions, limiting scalability and on-board deployment. We propose SemCityLoc, a semantic-geometric alignment system that reframes aerial pose estimation as structured surface registration between foundation-model-derived visual priors and standardized LoD-compliant 3D city models. Instead of matching sparse contours or dense texture, our method aligns semantic surfaces and monocular depth with lightweight semantic 3D building models, increasing pose discriminability in repetitive and occluded urban environments. To enable accurate evaluation, we introduce SemCityLockeD, the first real-world benchmark combining centimeter-accurate UAV poses with standardized LoD1--LoD3 semantic city models and challenging low-altitude imagery. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements over existing map-based approaches, improving recall by up to 36% and reducing mean positional error from 9.89m to 2.62m in challenging urban canyons. Our results indicate that semantically structured geometry provides sufficient and scalable constraints for high-precision aerial localization without radiometric scene reconstructions. The code and data are available at https://albertchen98.github.io/SemCityLoc.

</details>


### [6] [Fine-tuning a multimodal large language model for clinician-grade autism behavioral scoring from short home videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27484)
*Mohammadmahdi Honarmand,Parnian Azizian,Aaron Kline,Kae Nurge,Zerin Nasrin Tumpa,Saimourya Surabhi,Kaitlyn Dunlap,Yang Qian,Ali Kargarandehkordi,Sameer Neupane,Peter Washington,Dennis P. Wall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过对400个家庭视频进行低秩自适应微调Gemini 2.5 Pro，模型仅基于30个行为特征即可实现与临床医生相当甚至更优的自闭症诊断效果（准确率77%，AUC 86%），且作为零样本能力直接诊断F1提升53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）在美国儿童中发病率高达1/31，但中位诊断年龄超过4岁。利用家庭视频等易获取观测数据的人工智能管道有望实现更早诊断，从而及时开展早期干预治疗。

Method: 在400个临床医生评分的家庭视频上，使用低秩自适应（LoRA）微调Gemini 2.5 Pro，仅训练30个行为特征（这些特征此前已验证可产生可靠的机器学习预测结果）。在99名保留儿童（49名ASD，50名神经典型发育）上进行评估。

Result: 与临床医生的评分者间信度（逐特征加权Cohen's kappa）提升40%（p<0.001），28个可评估特征中27个改善。零样本直接诊断F1提升53%（p<0.001），达到或超过临床医生水平。基于微调LLM行为特征的分类器管道在所有测试路径中与临床医生评分的输入匹配，准确率77%（95% CI: 68-85%），AUC 86%（95% CI: 78-92%）。

Conclusion: 微调多模态大语言模型可作为可扩展的行为特征提取器，用于自闭症评估和诊断。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects 1 in 31 US children, yet median age at diagnosis exceeds four years. Artificial intelligence pipelines that provide quantified diagnosis using easy to access observational data (e.g., home videos) could help with earlier diagnosis, and timely delivery of early treatments. We fine-tuned Gemini 2.5 Pro on 400 clinician-rated home videos with low-rank adaptation, training only on 30 behavioral features previously validated to produce reliable predictions when passed to various ML models. On 99 held-out children (49 ASD, 50 neurotypical), inter-rater reliability with clinicians (per-feature weighted Cohen's kappa) improved by 40% (p<0.001), with 27 of 28 evaluable features improving. As an emergent zero-shot capability, direct ASD diagnosis F1 improved by 53% (p<0.001), matching or exceeding clinician outcomes. Classifier-assisted pipelines using fine-tuned LLM-derived behavioral features matched clinician-scored inputs across all tested pathways and achieved 77% accuracy (95% CI: 68-85%) and an AUC of 86% (95% CI: 78-92%). Fine-tuned multimodal LLMs can serve as scalable behavioral feature extractors for use in autism assessment and diagnosis.

</details>


### [7] [SelectAnyTree: A Promptable Instance Segmentation Model for 3D Forest LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27491)
*Trung Thanh Nguyen,Daniel Lusk,Kilian Gerberding,Janusch Vajna-Jehle,Tuan-Anh Vu,Duc Viet Le,Tu Vo,Phi Le Nguyen,Yasutomo Kawanishi,Takahiro Komamizu,Ichiro Ide,Julian Frey,Teja Kattenborn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SelectAnyTree 是一种基于提示的森林点云实例分割模型，通过少量点击即可分割任意单木，在跨区域泛化中表现出色，以78.2 IoU（单次点击）远超现有方法，且参数量和推理时间更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林LiDAR点云实例分割面临标注稀缺的瓶颈，单公顷数据包含数百万点和数百棵重叠树冠，人工标注费力且易错。现有自动预分割方法缺乏交互式或AI辅助修正能力，导致标注成本仍然高昂。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) Click-to-query提示编码器，将每次点击转为包含3D位置、正/负极性和局部骨干特征的内容查询；(2) 冠层高度模型（CHM）引导的首个提示，无需用户输入即可提供树顶作为几何和生态学先验。最终提示查询通过状态空间查询解码器解码，以线性时间复杂度高效捕获大规模森林场景的长程上下文。

Result: 在7个不同森林区域和独立测试集上评估，单次点击达到78.2 IoU，比最强基线高24.8点；以最少点击达成所有精度目标；参数量和推理时间均少于先前的提示模型。

Conclusion: SelectAnyTree验证了提示式分割范式在森林点云实例分割中的有效性，为低成本、高精度的森林三维测量提供了可行方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated instance segmentation of forest LiDAR point clouds is increasingly critical as forest monitoring moves toward scalable, detailed, 3D measurement. Yet, progress is constrained by label scarcity for tree instances; a single hectare can hold millions of points and hundreds of overlapping, complex crowns, making manual annotation from scratch with raw data laborious and error-prone. Annotations are often corrected from automatic pre-segmentations, but remain costly as these provide no interactive or AI-assisted refinement. Inspired by the promptable paradigm of foundation segmentation models, we propose SelectAnyTree, a promptable instance segmentation model that delineates any individual tree in a 3D forest point cloud from a few clicks. It introduces two key components: Click-to-query prompt encoder and Canopy Height Model (CHM)-guided first prompt. The former turns each click into a single content query, encoding its 3D position and positive/negative polarity together with a pooled local backbone feature. The latter provides treetops as a geometry- and ecologically guided first prompt without any user input. The resulting prompt query is then decoded into one tree mask by a state-space query decoder to efficiently capture long-range context in large-scale forest scenes with linear-time complexity. We evaluate SelectAnyTree in interactive and instance-level settings across seven diverse forest regions and an independent held-out test dataset, demonstrating strong generalization beyond the training domains. It segments a target tree to 78.2 Intersection over Union (IoU) from a single click, 24.8 points above the strongest promptable baseline, and reaches every accuracy target with the fewest clicks, while using far fewer parameters and less inference time than prior promptable models. The source code is available at https://github.com/thanhhff/SelectAnyTree.

</details>


### [8] [DMV-Bench: Diagnosing Long-Horizon Multimodal Agents' Visual Memory with Incidental Cue Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27499)
*Yujin Tang,Chenming Shang,Ruize Xu,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMV-Bench是首个针对多模态智能体视觉记忆的交互式基准测试，基于1000种家居产品变体的电商目录构建，通过文本泄露契约确保区分信号仅存在于像素中。作者提出DualMem架构，基于双编码理论并行维护视觉和语言编码，在多种链长设置下均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆研究几乎完全聚焦于文本记忆，缺乏在交互环境中评估智能体对视觉信息（而非文本记录）记忆能力的基准。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 构建DMV-Bench基准：基于1000种家居产品变体的电商目录，每个产品图像带有独特预渲染的偶然线索，智能体需在自主购物会话中回忆特定线索产品并导航到其URL。提出DualMem架构，基于双编码理论并行维护视觉和语言两种编码通道。

Result: DualMem在Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，在链长J∈{5,10,15,50}的所有设置下均优于caption基线和三种最新的多模态智能体记忆系统。领先优势在控制记忆库大小和编码位置偏差后仍然存在。

Conclusion: 非对称双编码机制（视觉端到端承载线索，语言通道仅起查询定位的辅助作用）是有效的，视觉记忆在多模态智能体中具有不可替代的作用。

Abstract: Research on agent memory has matured rapidly, but almost entirely on the text side: few existing benchmarks ask, in an interactive environment, when an agent genuinely needs to remember what it saw rather than what it could write down. We introduce DMV-Bench (Code: https://github.com/yyyujintang/DMV-Bench), the first interactive benchmark for multimodal-agent visual memory. DMV-Bench is built on a controlled home-furnishing e-commerce catalogue of 1,000 product variants in which a text-leakage contract keeps the discriminative signal of each task in the pixels alone. Across a chain of autonomous shopping sessions, every visited product image carries a unique, pre-rendered incidental cue, and the agent is later asked to recall a particular cued product and navigate to its URL. Inspired by dual-coding theory, we propose DualMem, a memory architecture that maintains a visual and a verbal code in parallel. On DMV-Bench, DualMem outperforms a caption baseline and three recent multimodal agent-memory systems at every chain length J in {5, 10, 15, 50} on both Gemini 2.5 Flash and Qwen2.5-VL-7B, with the lead surviving controls for memory-bank size and encoding-position bias, and an asymmetric dual-coding regime in which vision carries the cue end-to-end while the verbal channel plays a smaller query-grounding role.

</details>


### [9] [Aloe-Vision: Robust Vision-Language Models for Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27500)
*Jaume Guasch-Martí,Enrique Lopez-Cuena,Martín Suárez-Fernández,Jordi Bayarri-Planas,Anna Arias-Duart,Dario Garcia-Gasulla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Aloe-Vision系列医疗大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs），通过构建大规模、高质量过滤的数据混合集Aloe-Vision-Data并公开完整权重、训练配方和数据，在7B和72B两种规模上训练模型，在保持通用能力的同时显著提升医疗领域性能，同时引入CareQA-Vision基准以支持可靠评估，并揭示当前LVLMs在临床环境中仍易受对抗性输入攻击的可靠性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域LVLMs的发展受限于三个关键问题：高质量医学多模态数据的稀缺性、安全关键场景下的鲁棒性担忧，以及评估基准的狭窄性和潜在污染问题，因此迫切需要完全开放和可复现的系统。

Method: 1) 构建Aloe-Vision-Data大规模质量过滤多模态/纯文本混合数据集；2) 基于该数据集训练Aloe-Vision模型（7B和72B两个规模），完全开源权重、训练配方和数据；3) 引入CareQA-Vision基准（基于西班牙MIR/EIR住院医师考试）进行可靠评估。

Result: 高质量训练混合能够产生平衡的LVLMs，在保持通用能力不妥协的前提下显著优于基线模型，与当前最先进替代方案相比具有竞争力；同时发现当前LVLMs仍易受对抗性和误导性输入的影响。

Conclusion: 开放和可复现的医疗LVLMs系统是推动领域进步的关键，但模型在临床环境中的鲁棒性和可靠性仍需进一步改进，尤其是在面对对抗性攻击时。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) specialized in healthcare are emerging as a promising research direction due to their potential impact in clinical and biomedical applications. However, progress is constrained by the scarcity of high-quality medical multimodal data, concerns about robustness in safety-critical settings, and the narrow and potentially contaminated evaluation benchmarks that limit reliable assessment. To address these issues, the field requires state-of-the-art solutions to be fully open and reproducible systems in which all components can be inspected, evaluated, and improved. This work introduces Aloe-Vision-Data, a large-scale, quality-filtered mixture which integrates both medical and general domains across multimodal and text-only sources, designed for direct use in model fine-tuning. Building on this dataset, we train the Aloe-Vision family of medical LVLMs, openly released with full weights, training recipes and data, in two scales (7B and 72B). Through comprehensive benchmarking, we demonstrate that high quality training mixtures produce balanced LVLMs which yield significant gains over the baseline models without compromising general capabilities, achieving competitive performance with respect to state-of-the-art alternatives. To support reliable evaluation, we introduce CareQA-Vision, a carefully curated vision benchmark derived from MIR and EIR exams, the residency entrance exams for medical and nursing specialists in Spain, offering novel vision questions with low likelihood of contamination. Finally, we show that current LVLMs remain vulnerable to adversarial and misleading inputs, underscoring reliability challenges in clinical contexts.

</details>


### [10] [ReWorld: Learning Better Representations for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27504)
*Tianze Xia,Lijun Zhou,Kaixin Xiong,Jingfeng Yao,Yu Zhu,Zhenxin Zhu,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Wenyu Liu,Haiyang Sun,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReWorld，首个面向自动驾驶世界动作模型（WAM）的表征学习框架。核心思想是将WAM中的中间表征作为优化目标，沿三个维度进行塑造：对视频DiT的中间表征施加未来预测监督，对动作DiT的中间表征进行跨模态对齐和安全关键边界判别性塑造。实验表明，ReWorld在nuScenes和NAVSIM上显著提升了视频生成质量（FVD降低23.9%）、闭环规划性能（PDMS从89.1提升至90.4）并加速了约2倍的训练收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界动作模型（WAM）的研究主要关注模型架构设计，但对于WAM如何高效学习更好的世界表征以服务于规划任务这一问题尚未充分探索。标准训练仅监督生成模块和规划模块的输出端，而承载世界知识的中间表征仅作为拟合输出的副产品被间接塑造，缺乏直接优化。

Method: ReWorld将WAM中的中间表征作为直接优化目标，沿三个互补维度进行塑造：(1) 对负责生成的Video DiT的中间表征施加未来预测监督；(2) 对负责规划的动作DiT的中间表征进行跨模态对齐，使其与视频世界表征对齐；(3) 通过困难负样本监督进一步塑造动作DiT中间表征在安全关键边界附近的判别性。

Result: 在nuScenes和NAVSIM数据集上：微调后的视频生成FVD从81.3降至61.9（提升23.9%）；闭环PDMS从89.1提升至90.4，无需强化学习或后处理等后训练；从零开始的训练收敛速度提升约2倍。

Conclusion: 通过直接优化中间表征而非仅监督输出端，ReWorld有效提升了自动驾驶世界动作模型在视频生成质量和规划性能上的表现，并为该领域提供了一种高效的表征学习范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) model future environment evolution under action conditioning, offering a scalable paradigm for autonomous driving. However, existing approaches focus largely on model architecture design, and how a WAM can efficiently learn better world representations for planning remains underexplored. To address this gap, we propose ReWorld, the first representation learning framework specifically designed for autonomous-driving world action models. In WAMs, standard training supervises only the output ends of the generation and planning modules, leaving the intermediate representations that carry world knowledge to be shaped only indirectly, as byproducts of fitting these outputs. The core idea of ReWorld is to treat intermediate representations as direct targets of optimization, shaping them along three complementary dimensions. On the Video DiT responsible for generation, we impose future-predictive supervision on its intermediate representations. On the Action DiT responsible for planning, we first align its intermediate representations cross-modally with the video world representation, then further shape them to be discriminative around safety-critical boundaries via hard-negative supervision. In addition, we systematically analyze the effectiveness of existing representation learning methods in video generation world models, and discuss why their performance is limited on this task. Experiments on nuScenes and NAVSIM show that ReWorld improves fine-tuned video generation by 23.9% in FVD (81.3 to 61.9), raises closed-loop PDMS from 89.1 to 90.4 without any post-training such as RL or post-processing, and accelerates from-scratch convergence by approximately 2x.

</details>


### [11] [TruEye: Fine-Grained Detection of AI-Generated Human Subjects in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27505)
*Jay Barot,Dan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TruEye是一种针对AI生成或篡改图像的新型细粒度检测与定位模型，能区分五种合成内容类别（包括将真人合成到真实场景中的最复杂情况），采用掩码条件双流Transformer分离人体与场景token，无需LLM即可实现可解释预测，速度比基于LLM的方法快100倍以上，在6个数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器存在三个问题：1) 对训练时见过的生成器过拟合；2) 仅做不透明的二分类；3) 依赖昂贵的LLM来解释输出。需要一种更细粒度、可解释且高效的检测方法。

Method: 提出TruEye模型，核心是掩码条件双流Transformer：分离人体和场景token并保持patch级空间对应；每流内进行专门推理，通过区域门控交叉注意力强制主体与背景的语义一致性；采用token级监督和全局组合分类实现可解释预测，无需LLM。

Result: 在6个数据集和新构建的FineSyn数据集上，TruEye超越SOTA检测器，准确率更高、推理速度更快、对未见过的AI生成或篡改图像泛化能力更强；速度比基于LLM的方法快100倍以上。

Conclusion: TruEye首次实现了对五种合成内容类别的细粒度区分，特别是真人合成到真实场景中的最复杂情况，在不依赖LLM的情况下兼顾了准确性、效率和可解释性。

Abstract: AI generated images are proliferating across the Internet. While some are used for entertainment, others are weaponized for fraud and social engineering attacks on social media users. Existing detectors overfit to generators seen during training, treat detection as opaque binary classification, or rely on costly Large Language Models (LLMs) to explain their outputs. In this paper, we present TruEye, a novel model for fine grained detection and localization of AI manipulated or AI generated humans and scenes. Unlike conventional detectors that assign a single authenticity label, TruEye is the first to distinguish among five compositional categories of synthetic content, including the most challenging case in which a real human is composited into a real scene where they were never physically present. At its core is a mask conditioned dual stream transformer that separates human and scene tokens while preserving patch level spatial correspondence. Specialized reasoning within each stream and region gated cross attention enforce semantic coherence between subject and background, while token level supervision and global compositional classification yield robust, interpretable predictions without invoking an LLM. By restricting intra stream attention to semantically coherent tokens, TruEye also runs over $100\times$ faster than LLM based competitors. Experiments on 6 datasets and our newly curated FineSyn dataset, show that TruEye surpasses state of the art detectors with higher accuracy, faster inference, and stronger generalization to unseen AI generated or manipulated images.

</details>


### [12] [Structured-Li-GS: Structured 3D Gaussians Splatting with LiDAR Incorporation and Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27509)
*Huaiyuan Weng,Huibin Li,Chul Min Yeum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Structured-Li-GS，一种结合LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM的轻量级高斯泼溅框架，通过稠密彩色点云训练，利用子采样点云锚定高斯原语并从局部表面几何初始化椭球参数，集成多种损失函数实现无需高斯致密化的高质量三维重建，在保持适度模型大小的同时达到尺度准确的高保真结果，在基准数据集和自建数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅方法在三维重建中往往需要大量高斯原语导致模型庞大，且缺乏LiDAR数据的有效融合，难以同时实现高保真重建和模型轻量化。

Method: 1) 构建LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM框架生成准确、稠密的彩色点云；2) 使用子采样点云锚定高斯原语，从局部表面几何初始化椭球参数；3) 集成光度损失、扁平化损失、偏移损失、深度损失和法向损失等多重损失函数进行训练，无需高斯致密化操作。

Result: 在基准数据集和自建真实数据集上，Structured-Li-GS在使用更少高斯原语的情况下，超越了当前最先进方法的重建质量。

Conclusion: 融合LiDAR与高斯泼溅的结构化方法能够在减少模型规模的同时实现高精度、尺度准确的三维重建，验证了多传感器融合与几何先验初始化在高斯泼溅框架中的有效性。

Abstract: In this study, we develop a Structured framework for Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with LiDAR integration (Structured-Li-GS). It is a lightweight Gaussian Splatting pipeline that leverages LiDAR-inertial-visual SLAM. Structured-Li-GS achieves high-quality 3D reconstructions with fewer Gaussians by training on accurate, dense, colorized point clouds. Gaussian primitives are anchored using sub-sampled point clouds, and their ellipsoidal parameters are initialized from local surface geometry. Our training strategy integrates a comprehensive set of loss terms, including photometric, flattening, offset, depth, and normal losses, guided by the dense point cloud, enabling accurate reconstruction without Gaussian densification. This approach produces up-to-scale, high-fidelity results with a moderate model size. For experimental validation, we develop a custom hardware-synchronized LiDAR-camera handheld scanner. Experiments on both benchmark datasets and our real-world in-house dataset demonstrate that Structured-Li-GS surpasses state-of-the-art methods while using fewer Gaussians.

</details>


### [13] [Tessellating The Earth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27514)
*Daniel Cher,Hamza Iqbal,Eric Xing,Brian Wei,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Tessellating the Earth (TTE)，一种基于可学习球面Voronoi划分的地理位置编码器，通过可微分方式集中表征能力于关键区域，并引入全局语义令牌来桥接局部空间结构与全局语义理解，在多项地理空间分类与回归任务中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理编码器将坐标投影到固定基函数（如球谐函数）上，均匀分配表征能力，导致海洋与城市等不同区域被同等对待，无法集中资源到信息量丰富的区域。

Method: 构建可学习的球面Voronoi划分，每个Voronoi站点携带独立嵌入并在训练中向判别性区域迁移；引入全局语义令牌，从卫星图像中蒸馏语义知识形成紧凑词汇表，供编码器在推理时参考。

Result: 在地理空间分类与回归任务中达到新最优，作为地理先验用于iNaturalist-2018细粒度物种分类时取得最强结果。

Conclusion: TTE通过可学习Voronoi划分自适应分配表征资源，结合全局语义令牌实现了局部空间结构与全局语义的有效融合，验证了其在多种地理空间任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Geolocation encoders, which map geographic coordinates to learned representations, are emerging as an effective means of capturing visual and non-visual characteristics from a latitude-longitude pair alone. However, existing approaches project coordinates onto fixed bases (e.g., spherical harmonics), allocating representational capacity uniformly and devoting equal resources to the open ocean and to a developing city. We introduce Tessellating the Earth (TTE), a location encoder built from learnable Spherical Voronoi partitions that concentrates representational capacity where it is needed in a fully differentiable, end-to-end manner. Each Voronoi site carries its own embedding and migrates during training toward discriminative areas. To bridge the gap between local spatial structure and global semantic understanding, we introduce \emph{global semantic tokens}: a set of shared learnable concept tokens that distill semantic knowledge from the satellite imagery into a compact vocabulary the location encoder can reference at inference, enabling geographically distant sites covering similar environments to share semantics. TTE sets a new state of the art for location encoders across a suite of geospatial classification and regression tasks, and achieves the strongest results when used as a geographic prior for fine-grained species classification on iNaturalist-2018. Code, and weights are available at https://github.com/mvrl/TTE.

</details>


### [14] [Large Language Model Teaches Visual Students: Cross-Modality Transfer of Fine-Grained Conceptual Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27527)
*Thomas Shih-Chao Liang,Zhuoran Yu,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaViD提出了一种从纯语言大模型向纯视觉学生模型进行知识蒸馏的新框架，通过让LLM生成多项选择题来构建类别语义签名，无需多模态配对数据，在多个细粒度基准上优于现有方法，并提升了模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过大规模文本预训练获得了广泛的概念知识，但其监督其他模态模型的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。现有知识蒸馏方法通常依赖视觉语言模型或多模态配对数据，限制了语言模型知识的迁移。

Method: LaViD框架通过三个步骤实现语言到视觉的知识蒸馏：(1) 利用LLM为视觉类别生成描述性多项选择题；(2) 将每个类别映射到这些MCQ上的软标签分布，形成概念签名；(3) 在视觉学生模型训练中引入辅助蒸馏损失，使学生模型输出分布逼近语言教师生成的软标签分布。

Result: 在多个细粒度基准上，仅使用纯语言教师且不访问图像数据的LaViD，一致优于从视觉语言模型蒸馏的MaKD方法；与DKD、MLKD等最先进视觉蒸馏方法相比，表现相当或更优；在Waterbirds数据集上，最差组准确率大幅提升，显示出对虚假关联的鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: 纯语言模型的知识可以通过精心设计的问答形式有效迁移到视觉模型，为跨模态知识蒸馏提供了新范式，且无需依赖昂贵的多模态配对数据。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) possess broad conceptual knowledge acquired through large-scale text pretraining, yet their potential to supervise models in other modalities remains underexplored. In this work, we propose LaViD--Language-to-Visual Knowledge Distillation--a simple and effective framework for transferring high-level semantic knowledge from a language-only teacher to a vision-only student model. Instead of relying on paired multimodal data, LaViD elicits conceptual signals from an LLM by prompting it to generate multiple-choice questions (MCQs) that probe semantic distinctions between visual classes. Each class is mapped to a soft label distribution over these MCQs, forming a rich conceptual signature that guides the student through an auxiliary distillation loss. Notably, despite using a language-only teacher without access to image data, LaViD consistently outperforms recent methods like MaKD that distill from vision-language models across multiple fine-grained benchmarks. It also achieves competitive or superior performance compared to state-of-the-art visual distillation methods such as DKD and MLKD, with further gains when combined with logit standardization. On the Waterbirds dataset, LaViD substantially improves worst-group accuracy, demonstrating enhanced robustness to spurious correlations with distillation. Code is available at https://github.com/lliangthomas/lavid.

</details>


### [15] [MemoBench: Benchmarking World Modeling in Dynamically Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27537)
*Haoyu Chen,Kaichen Zhou,Hang Hua,Kaile Zhang,Jingwen Qian,Wufei Ma,Haonan Chen,Chunjiang Liu,Yizhou Zhao,Xiaoyuan Wang,Weiyue Li,Alan Yuille,Paul Pu Liang,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoBench基准，评估视频生成模型在动态环境中物体消失再出现后的记忆一致性，包含360个真实/合成视频片段和四维度评估体系，测试8个SOTA模型揭示关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准大多只评估物体在视野内的记忆一致性，少数评估物体出视野的基准仅考虑静态场景（遮挡期间无变化）。为填补这一空白，论文引入"消失-重现"范式，评估模型在动态变化环境中物体被遮挡后能否正确恢复其更新后的状态。

Method: 构建MemoBench基准：1）收集360个ground-truth视频片段（合成+真实场景）；2）设计四维度评估体系（自动化指标+VQA）；3）测试8个SOTA视频生成模型。

Result: 对8个SOTA模型的评估揭示了在"消失-重现"范式下记忆一致性的关键洞察和开放挑战。

Conclusion: 现有模型在物体消失再出现的动态场景中记忆一致性表现不足，MemoBench为这一方向提供了有效的诊断工具。

Abstract: Video generation models aspire to simulate dynamic environments, and several benchmarks now evaluate memory consistency across frames. However, most assess consistency only while the target remains in view, and the few that force objects out of view evaluate static scenes where nothing changes during occlusion. To bridge this gap, we introduce MemoBench, a diagnostic benchmark built around the disappear-and-reappear paradigm in dynamically changing environments: a target object undergoes a physical process, disappears from view, and must be correctly recovered in its updated state upon reappearance. We curate 360 ground-truth clips spanning synthetic and real-world scenes, and design an evaluation suite combining automated metrics with VQA-based assessment across four diagnostic pillars. Evaluation of eight state-of-the-art models reveals key insights and open challenges regarding memory consistency under the disappear-and-reappear paradigm.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond MoCap: Scaling Motion Tokenizers with Synthetic Human Motion for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27547)
*Yiwen Yan,Wanning He,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过大规模合成人体动作数据扩展运动表示空间，并重新设计VQ-VAE分词器以适应扩展后的运动空间，从而突破真实动作捕捉数据多样性不足的瓶颈，显著提升文本到动作、动作延续等下游任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作生成模型受限于动作捕捉数据集的多样性不足（多为常见重复动作，缺乏对长尾复杂动作的覆盖），导致学到的潜在表示中运动词汇受限，泛化到稀有、组合及高动态动作时表现不佳。

Method: 提出一个框架：(1) 构建数据生成流程，产生多样化、物理合理的合成动作序列，超出已有数据分布；(2) 重新设计VQ-VAE分词器，使其适应扩展后的运动空间，同时缩放训练分布和离散码本，捕捉更丰富的运动基元。

Result: 合成动作训练显著提升了所学运动词汇的覆盖率和组合性，在文本到动作和动作延续等任务上取得一致改进，且与MotionGPT等现有框架完全兼容。

Conclusion: 主要瓶颈在于所学运动表示的支持范围有限，而非模型架构本身；将合成动作缩放与表示学习相结合是实现更具表现力、可控性和泛化能力的人体运动合成的有效路径。

Abstract: Human motion generation models are fundamentally constrained by the limited diversity of motion capture datasets, which predominantly contain common, repetitive actions and fail to cover the long tail of complex human movements, resulting in a restricted motion vocabulary in learned latent representations and poor generalization to rare, compositional, and highly dynamic motions. In this work, we propose a framework for expanding the motion representation space by leveraging large-scale synthetic human motion, introducing a data generation pipeline that produces diverse, physically plausible motion sequences beyond the distribution of existing datasets and integrating it with a redesigned VQ-VAE tokenizer that adapts to this expanded motion space. Unlike conventional tokenizers trained on narrow data distributions, our approach jointly scales both the training distribution and the discrete codebook, enabling the model to capture a significantly richer set of motion primitives. We demonstrate that training with synthetic motion substantially improves the coverage and compositionality of the learned motion vocabulary, leading to consistent gains across motion generation tasks such as text-to-motion and motion continuation, while remaining fully compatible with existing frameworks including MotionGPT. Our results suggest that the primary bottleneck lies in the limited support of the learned motion representation, rather than model architecture alone. Scaling synthetic motion in tandem with representation learning offers a principled path toward more expressive, controllable, and generalizable human motion synthesis.

</details>


### [17] [Understanding Cross-Rig Generalization in Automotive Perception: a Multi-Rig Benchmark and Rig Variation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27554)
*Tim Alexander Bader,Tim Dieter Eberhardt,Maximilian Dillitzer,Wilhelm Stork*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Plentiful CARLA Camera Rigs基准测试，通过14种不同相机配置系统研究自动驾驶多视角感知模型在不同传感器布局间的跨域泛化问题，并引入两种基于标定的描述符（Rig Variance和Rig Contrastive Distance）量化几何差异与性能迁移的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实自动驾驶车队中相机位置、朝向、视场角和摄像头数量存在显著差异，而现有感知系统通常在固定传感器配置下开发与评估，导致跨传感器配置的领域差距被忽视，亟需系统性研究几何观测变化对模型泛化能力的影响。

Method: 1) 构建Plentiful CARLA Camera Rigs基准，在相同驾驶场景下使用14种系统设计的相机配置渲染数据；2) 提出两种校准描述符：Rig Variance（描述单个配置内部多样性）和Rig Contrastive Distance（度量不同配置间的几何差异）；3) 分析代表性多视角感知架构的跨配置迁移表现。

Result: 几何配置变化会导致显著的性能偏移；几何差异与相对跨配置性能变化强相关；Rig Contrastive Distance可作为预测传感器配置间迁移难度的可靠代理指标。

Conclusion: 相机几何配置差异是影响多视角感知模型泛化能力的关键因素，所提出的描述符可有效量化并预测跨配置迁移难度，为自动驾驶感知系统的鲁棒部署提供分析工具。

Abstract: Camera-based perception systems for autonomous driving are typically developed and evaluated using fixed sensor rigs, while real-world vehicle fleets exhibit substantial variation in camera placement, orientation, field of view, and camera count. This mismatch introduces a cross-rig domain gap in which only the geometric observation process changes. To study this effect under controlled conditions, we introduce Plentiful CARLA Camera Rigs, a benchmark that renders identical driving scenes under 14 systematically designed camera rigs. This setup enables direct analysis of cross-rig generalization without confounding changes in scene content or appearance. Using the benchmark, we analyze cross-rig transfer behavior of representative multi-view perception architectures and observe substantial performance shifts induced by geometric rig variation. To facilitate structured analysis, we further introduce two calibration-based descriptors derived from rig metadata: Rig Variance, capturing internal rig diversity, and Rig Contrastive Distance, measuring geometric discrepancy between rigs. Our experiments show that geometric rig differences strongly correlate with relative cross-rig performance shifts and that Rig Contrastive Distance provides a reliable proxy for ranking transfer difficulty between sensor rigs.

</details>


### [18] [Radar Guided Camera Verification for Automatic Emergency Braking Rethinking Object Detection in Radar Camera Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27556)
*Ram Charan Akula,Sivanathan Kandhasamy,Manikandan Ganesan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练数据、模型权重或GPU加速的雷达引导边缘密度门控方法，将AEB系统的相机搜索空间减少98.7%，平均处理延迟0.121ms/ROI，AUC 0.898，召回率0.994，在33个威胁场景中实现零漏刹车。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AEB系统依赖计算密集型的目标检测器进行视觉确认，而雷达已定位目标后，相机只需验证障碍物存在而非完整识别，因此需要更高效的方法。

Method: 提出雷达引导的边缘密度门控方法：在雷达指示的图像感兴趣区域（ROI）内，基于边缘密度进行障碍物验证，无需训练数据、模型权重或GPU加速，并集成到完整的雷达-相机融合AEB系统中。

Result: 在72次驾驶会话、131,603帧图像中，相机搜索空间减少98.7%，平均处理延迟0.121ms/ROI，AUC 0.898，召回率0.994；33个威胁场景中零漏刹车。

Conclusion: 该方法在极低计算开销下实现了高精度的障碍物验证，无需训练数据和GPU，适合低成本部署，且在真实场景中AEB系统表现可靠。

Abstract: Radar camera fusion is widely used in Automatic Emergency Braking AEB systems because radar provides reliable range and velocity measurements while cameras provide a proper visual confirmation of the objects . Most of the deployed systems perform this confirmation using computationally intensive object detectors. However, if the radar has already localized a target, the camera may only need to verify the obstacles presence rather than solving a full problem by identifying the object. Our work proposes a radar scoped edge density gate that performs obstacle verification within radar guided image regions of interest. This method requires no training data, model weights, or GPU acceleration and was integrated into a complete radar camera fusion AEB system with brake by wire actuation. Evaluated on a real instrumented vehicle across 72 driving sessions and 131,603 camera frames, the proposed approach reduced the camera search space by up to 98.7 percentage, achieved a mean processing latency of 0.121 ms per ROI, an AUC of 0.898, and a recall of 0.994. Across 33 staged threat scenarios, the complete AEB system recorded zero missed brake events.

</details>


### [19] [Perceptual 3D Simulation With Physical World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27575)
*Wanhee Lee,Klemen Kotar,Rahul Mysore Venkatesh,Jared Watrous,Daniel L. K. Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P3Sim是一个物理世界建模系统，结合学习到的物理世界模型、几何条件模块和持久场景记忆，在部分观测和不完整3D变换信号下模拟未来场景状态，适用于新视角合成、物体操作和动态场景预测等多种3D变换任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界系统必须依赖部分且不完整的感知输入和局部动作来预测场景在3D变换后的演变，这与理想模拟器拥有完整3D几何和动力学信息的情况不同，因此需要一种能在不完整信息下进行场景模拟的方法。

Method: P3Sim由三个交互组件构成：1) 学习到的物理世界模型，将感知视为对多模态场景变量的概率推断；2) 几何条件模块，提供部分3D变换信号以在推理时条件化世界模型；3) 持久场景记忆，随时间整合预测，支持在线更新和不确定性下的一致性。

Result: P3Sim在多种3D变换任务（如新视角合成、物体操作和动态场景预测）中展现出良好的泛化能力，有效平衡了数据驱动的灵活性与内置归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 通过将学习推理与显式几何结构相结合，P3Sim作为一种灵活的感知模拟器，推动了向通用3D场景理解与转换的进展。

Abstract: Predicting how a scene will evolve after a desired 3D transformation from images is a central goal in vision, graphics, and robotics. Yet unlike ideal simulators with full access to 3D geometry and dynamics, real world systems must rely on perceptual inputs and local actions that are inherently partial and incomplete. In this work, we present P3Sim, a physical world modeling system that simulates future scene states under both partial observations and incomplete 3D transformation signals. P3Sim is composed of three interacting components: a learned physical world model, a geometric conditioning module, and a persistent scene memory. The world model interprets perception as probabilistic inference over multimodal scene variables, providing predictions of the distributions of any scene variable conditioned on any combination of others. The geometric conditioning module provides a partial 3D transform signal for conditioning the world model at inference time. The persistent scene memory integrates predictions over time, enabling online updates and consistency under uncertainty. By combining learned inference with explicit geometric structure, P3Sim balances data-driven flexibility with built-in inductive bias. This design yields a flexible perceptual simulator that generalizes across diverse 3D transformation tasks, such as novel view synthesis, object manipulation, and dynamic scene prediction, advancing toward general purpose 3D scene understanding and transformation.

</details>


### [20] [DeLux: Cross-Modal Local Artifact Restoration in Video Using Neuromorphic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27576)
*Bartosz Stachowiak,Dariusz Brzezinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeLux，一种利用神经形态事件流作为结构先验引导RGB视频中光照伪影（眩光、过曝等）检测与修复的跨模态复原范式，在合成和真实数据上取得显著效果，MS-SSIM超0.99，伪影严重度降低88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RGB相机受光照伪影（眩光、过曝等）影响导致不可逆信息损失，现有方法孤立处理各类伪影，无法恢复被复杂空间离散退化完全遮挡的结构细节。

Method: 提出DeLux模块化pipeline，利用神经形态事件流作为结构先验，引导RGB视频中光照伪影的检测与修复。

Result: 在合成基准和真实自动驾驶数据上，DeLux有效抑制局部伪影并恢复受损区域，平均MS-SSIM超0.99，真实汽车场景中伪影严重度降低88%。

Conclusion: 跨模态复原范式优于现有RGB基线及事件引导HDR模型，并公开了合成伪影生成工具和真实评估数据集以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Conventional RGB cameras suffer from lighting artifacts such as flare, glare, flicker, and overexposure, leading to irrecoverable information loss that necessitates computational restoration. However, existing approaches treat these problems in isolation, failing to recover structural details completely obscured by complex spatially discrete image degradations. In this paper, we propose a novel cross-modal restoration paradigm and present DeLux, a modular proof-of-concept pipeline that leverages neuromorphic event streams as a structural prior to guide the targeted detection and inpainting of lighting artifacts in RGB video. Validation on synthetic benchmarks and real-world automotive footage demonstrates that DeLux effectively suppresses local artifacts and restores affected regions. The proposed approach outperforms existing RGB-only baselines and event-guided HDR models, achieving an average MS-SSIM of over 0.99 across all artifact types and demonstrating up to an 88% reduction in artifact severity in real-world automotive footage. The synthetic artifact generation tools and curated real-world evaluation datasets are made publicly available to foster future research on cross-modal restoration.

</details>


### [21] [Distribution-based deep multiple instance learning for tumor proportion scoring in NSCLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27579)
*Krzysztof Pysz,Artur Bartczak,Jarosław Kwiecień,Piotr Krajewski,Witold Dyrka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于零膨胀贝塔分布的多实例学习框架，用于非小细胞肺癌肿瘤比例评分的全幻灯片级别预测，仅使用幻灯片级标签即可实现高精度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非小细胞肺癌中肿瘤比例评分的准确评估对治疗规划和预后至关重要，但存在人工标注繁琐、认证专家稀缺等问题。现有MIL方法在处理非表达性（零类）图像时表现不佳。

Method: 提出两阶段端到端框架：(1) 嵌入提取与多分类网络，捕获每个斑块的病理特征；(2) MIL模型聚合嵌入，预测零膨胀贝塔分布参数，表征整个幻灯片的TPS概率分布。

Result: 零膨胀贝塔建模显著优于基线线性回归和岭回归，并通过分布集中度捕获了预期的预测精度。

Conclusion: 该分布导向的端到端MIL框架在仅使用幻灯片级标签的情况下，有效提升了TPS预测的准确性和可解释性，为临床诊断提供了更可靠的辅助工具。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of tumor proportion score (TPS) in non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) is critical for treatment planning and prognosis. Key challenges include the tedious manual work required to annotate each slide, combined with the limited number of experts certified for this task. Multiple instance learning (MIL) has proven to be an effective approach for predicting TPS scores at the slide level; however, existing methods struggle with non-expressive (zero class) images. Our approach involves two models: (1) an embedding-extraction and multiclass-classification network that captures the histopathological features of individual patches, and (2) a MIL model that aggregates these embeddings to predict zero-inflated beta (ZIBeta) parameters representing the overall TPS probability distribution for the entire slide. Using only slide-level TPS scores as labels, we demonstrate how this end-to-end framework can leverage a novel distribution-based architecture to improve prediction accuracy and explainability. ZIBeta modeling significantly outperforms baseline linear and ridge regression while capturing expected accuracy through distribution concentration.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Points: Spherical Distributional Part Prototypes for Interpretable Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27582)
*Duarte Leão,Diogo Pereira Araújo,Catarina Barata,Carlos Santiago*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: vMFProto提出了一种基于von Mises-Fisher分布的原型网络框架，通过分布化建模替代传统的点原型，捕获部件级可变性，并结合熵最优传输进行结构化分配，在多个细粒度数据集上实现了解释质量和鲁棒性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于点原型的可解释神经网络在归一化、方向性的嵌入空间中，由于语义部件存在显著的类内可变性，导致点原型冗余或不稳定，损害了解释质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 将每个类别建模为超球面上的von Mises-Fisher混合分布，每个原型学习自身的浓度参数以捕获部件级可变性；2) 使用熵最优传输(OT)实现结构化的patch-to-prototype分配；3) 两阶段训练：OT驱动的原型发现 + 端到端微调（含patch级蒸馏和分布感知多样性正则化）。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011、Stanford Dogs和Stanford Cars数据集上，使用冻结的DINO骨干网络，vMFProto在解释质量（一致性、稳定性、独特性）上达到SOTA，同时保持有竞争力的分类精度。

Conclusion: vMFProto通过分布化原型建模和最优传输分配，有效解决了点原型的冗余和不稳定问题，生成了局部化、非冗余的部件级证据，为可解释原型网络提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Prototype-based neural networks aim to provide intrinsic interpretability by grounding predictions in a small set of part prototypes. However, modern vision backbones typically operate in normalized, directional embedding spaces where each semantic part exhibits substantial intra-class variability. As a result, point prototypes often become redundant or unstable, hurting both explanation quality and robustness. We propose vMFProto, a distributional part-prototype framework that models each class as a mixture of von Mises-Fisher components on the hypersphere. Each prototype learns its own concentration, capturing part-specific variability, and we use entropic optimal transport (OT) to obtain structured patch-to-prototype assignments. A two-stage training schedule performs OT-driven prototype discovery followed by end-to-end refinement with patch-level distillation and distribution-aware diversity regularization. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, Stanford Dogs, and Stanford Cars with frozen DINO backbones show that vMFProto achieves state-of-the-art explanation quality (consistency, stability, and distinctiveness) with competitive accuracy. Qualitative results confirm that vMFProto yields localized, non-redundant part evidence.

</details>


### [23] [CoIn: Comprehensive 2D-3D Inpainting with Gaussian Splatting Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27584)
*Hana Kim,Minje Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoIn是一个通过多阶段一致性流水线桥接2D修复模型和3DGS的新框架，利用双向信息流（2D→3D和3D→2D）实现了灵活掩码下的物体移除与插入，在3D场景修复任务上达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的3D编辑方法依赖精确的多视图分割掩码，且局限于物体移除任务，无法处理任意形状掩码和物体插入等多样化任务。

Method: 1) 使用扩散模型生成初始修复图像，支持任意形状掩码和物体插入；2) 提出参考自适应GS与特征注意力，通过自适应加权参考视图重建粗3D场景（2D→3D）；3) 通过GS参考特征扭曲为扩散过程提供几何引导，确保多视图一致性（3D→2D）；4) 纹理增强鉴别器精炼3D场景以提升光度真实感（2D→3D）。

Result: 实验表明CoIn有效利用双向信息流，在物体移除和插入任务上均达到最先进性能，且支持灵活掩码输入。

Conclusion: CoIn通过双向信息流动的多阶段一致性流水线，突破了现有方法在掩码形式和任务类型上的限制，实现了更通用的3D场景修复。

Abstract: 3D scene inpainting is essential for reconstructing areas corrupted by occlusions or limited viewpoints. While recent methods leverage Gaussian Splatting (GS) for efficient 3D editing, they often depend on precise multi-view segmentation masks and are inherently constrained to object removal tasks. We propose CoIn, a novel framework that bridges 2D inpainting models and 3DGS through a multi-stage consistency pipeline. Our approach first generates initial inpainted images using a diffusion model, enabling the use of arbitrary-shaped masks and diverse tasks like object insertion. We then introduce Reference Adaptive GS with Feature Attention to reconstruct a coarse 3D scene by adaptively weighing towards a reference view (2D -> 3D). This 3D representation provides geometric guidance to the diffusion process via GS-based Reference Feature Warping, ensuring multi-view consistency (3D -> 2D). Finally, a Texture-Enhancing Discriminator refines the 3D scene to achieve high photometric realism (2D -> 3D). Experiments show that CoIn, effectively leveraging bidirectional information flow, achieves state-of-the-art performance and effectively handles both object removal and object insertion with flexible mask input.

</details>


### [24] [Dismantling Pathological Shortcuts: A Causal Framework for Faithful LVLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27596)
*Liu Yu,Can Chen,Ping Kuang,Zhikun Feng,Fan Zhou,Gillian Dobbie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fox通过诊断视觉注意力结构错位并实施因果干预，在无需训练的情况下有效缓解LVLM的对象幻觉问题，性能比SID提升29.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM虽具备复杂推理能力，但仍存在对象幻觉问题。现有方法多基于注意力强度假设，未能揭示幻觉产生的深层动态结构错位机制。

Method: 提出Fox框架：(1) 利用视觉注意力熵探针无监督定位风险中介头；(2) 通过数值logit饱和实施靶向因果干预，切断病理捷径；(3) 采用冲突门控协同解码策略，平衡干预的忠实性与观测的流畅性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上达到SOTA，比SID方法提升29.1%，同时保持语言丰富性。

Conclusion: 揭示了幻觉产生的关键机制——风险中介头在决策关键步脱离视觉证据锁定语言先验，形成绕过视觉锚定的病理捷径。Fox通过训练无关的推理时干预有效解决该问题。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) exhibit sophisticated reasoning but remain susceptible to object hallucination. Deviating from the prevailing attention intensity assumption, we reveal a deeper dynamic structural misalignment: hallucination is triggered at decision-critical steps where specific attention heads, acting as risky mediators, decouple from visual evidence to lock onto language priors. This establishes a pathological shortcut that bypasses visual grounding. To dismantle this, we propose Fox (Faithfulness and Observational-flow via eXpression-rectification), a training-free inference-time framework. Fox diagnoses structural misalignment using a visual attention entropy probe to localize risky mediators unsupervisedly. We then execute a targeted causal intervention via numerical logit saturation to physically sever the shortcut path. Finally, a conflict-gated cooperative decoding strategy reconciles interventional faithfulness with observational fluency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Fox achieves SOTA performance, outperforming SID by 29.1% while preserving linguistic richness. Code is available at https://github.com/Cc2021start/Fox.

</details>


### [25] [Qwen-Image-2.0-RL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27608)
*Yixian Xu,Kaiyuan Gao,Yuxiang Chen,Yilei Chen,Zecheng Tang,Zihao Liu,Zikai Zhou,Deqing Li,Hao Meng,Kuan Cao,Jiahao Li,Jie Zhang,Liang Peng,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Shengming Yin,Tianhe Wu,Xiaoyue Chen,Yan Shu,Yanran Zhang,Yi Wang,Yu Wu,Yujia Wu,Zekai Zhang,Zhendong Wang,Xiao Xu,Kun Yan,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-Image-2.0-RL，一种基于强化学习人类反馈（RLHF）和在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的后训练流程，通过任务特定的复合奖励模型和GRPO强化学习框架，显著提升了文本到图像生成与图像编辑任务的视觉质量和指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在预训练后仍存在视觉质量不足和指令遵循能力有限的问题，亟需一种有效的后训练方法，在保持预训练知识的同时提升模型在特定任务上的表现。

Method: 构建任务特定的复合奖励模型（涵盖对齐、美学、肖像保真度等维度），采用点式评分范式和思维链推理；开发基于GRPO的强化学习训练框架，包含混合无分类器引导策略、组内奖励范围过滤和逐类别奖励权重校准；最终通过在线策略蒸馏，将多个任务专用策略模型合并为单一学生模型。

Result: 在Qwen-Image-Bench上总体得分57.84（+2.61），文本到图像竞技场Elo评分1193（+78），图像编辑竞技场Elo评分1349（+93），在美学质量、提示遵循度和编辑准确性上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: RLHF结合在线策略蒸馏是一种有效的扩散模型后训练范式，能够在不牺牲模型通用能力的前提下提升特定任务的性能。

Abstract: We present Qwen-Image-2.0-RL, a post-training pipeline that applies reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and on-policy distillation (OPD) to improve both the visual quality and instruction-following capability of the Qwen-Image-2.0 diffusion model. To provide reliable reward signals, we construct task-specific composite reward models by fine-tuning vision-language models with a pointwise scoring paradigm and chain-of-thought reasoning. For text-to-image generation, the reward models cover alignment, aesthetics, and portrait fidelity dimensions. For image editing tasks, the reward system addresses instruction-following accuracy and face identity preservation. Building on this reward system, we develop a scalable GRPO-based RL training framework, incorporating a hybrid classifier-free guidance (CFG) strategy to preserve pre-trained knowledge, prompt curation via intra-group reward range filtering, and per-category reward weight calibration. To merge the task-specialized RL policies for T2I and editing, we propose on-policy distillation as the final training stage, which consolidates multiple teachers into a single student model through trajectory-level velocity matching. Extensive evaluation shows that Qwen-Image-2.0-RL achieves 57.84 overall score on Qwen-Image-Bench (+2.61 over the base model), Elo ratings of 1193 in text-to-image arena (+78) and 1349 in image edit arena (+93), demonstrating consistent gains in aesthetic quality, prompt adherence, and editing accuracy.

</details>


### [26] [Denoising ICF Images with Multiplicative Uniform Noise: A Self-Supervised Study Based on the Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27635)
*Gyeongha Hwang,Bradley Thomas Wolfe,Naima Naheed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了对数域Noisier2Inverse自监督去噪框架，用于处理惯性约束聚变（ICF）图像中的乘性均匀噪声。理论证明了对数域自监督损失最小化等价于变换域的有监督学习。实验结果表明，该方法在PSNR和SSIM指标上显著优于BM3D和Noise2Self等对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICF图像受到乘性均匀噪声的严重污染，传统有监督去噪方法需要大量干净参考数据，而实际中难以获取。现有的自监督方法（如Noise2Self）和经典滤波方法（如BM3D）在ICF图像上表现不佳，亟需一种适应ICF图像特性且无需干净数据的自监督去噪框架。

Method: 提出Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse框架，将对数域变换与Noisier2Inverse自监督策略相结合。核心理论贡献是证明了在对数域中最小化自监督损失等价于在变换域中进行有监督学习。设计了两种变体：变体A使用固定高斯噪声加载，变体B使用逐图像JSON均匀噪声加载。针对ICF图像的独特特征解决了多个实现挑战。

Result: 变体B达到最佳效果：平均PSNR 21.41dB，SSIM 0.8358，相比含噪基线（1.95dB）提升+19.46dB；变体A达到PSNR 21.39dB，SSIM 0.8436。显著优于BM3D对数域（4.47dB，SSIM 0.5181）和Noise2Self（4.75dB，SSIM 0.0177）。

Conclusion: Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse框架在ICF图像乘性均匀噪声去除任务上表现出色，两种变体均大幅超越现有方法。该框架完全自监督，训练过程无需干净数据，适用于难以获取干净参考图像的实际应用场景。

Abstract: This paper documents the implementation and evaluation of a self-supervised denoising framework on Inertial Confinement Fusion (ICF) images corrupted by Multiplicative Uniform noise: the \emph{Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse} framework. This framework is developed and analysed in this work; the key theoretical result -- that minimising the log-domain self-supervised loss is equivalent to supervised learning in the transformed domain -- is presented with full proof. We document significant implementation challenges arising from the unique characteristics of ICF imagery, describe the fixes applied at each stage, and report final quantitative results. The log-domain approach with per-image JSON Uniform noise loading (Variant~B) achieves the best result: a mean PSNR of $21.41\db$ and SSIM of $0.8358$, a $+19.46\db$ improvement over the noisy input baseline of $1.95\db$, substantially outperforming BM3D log-domain ($4.47\db$, SSIM $0.5181$) and Noise2Self ($4.75\db$, SSIM $0.0177$). Variant~A, using fixed Gaussian noise loading, achieves $21.39\db$ PSNR and SSIM $0.8436$. Of the three evaluated methods, Log-Domain Noisier2Inverse and Noise2Self are entirely self-supervised during training, requiring no clean ground truth data; BM3D is a classical filter-based method requiring no training at all. The clean reference images are used solely for quantitative evaluation of all three methods.

</details>

*Xuan-Bach Mai,Hoang-Minh Nguyen-Huu,Quoc-Nghia Nguyen,Hoang-Tung Vu,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合多种检测方法的鲁棒策略，用于区分AI生成图像与真实图像，在CIFAKE数据集上达到97.32%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用融合策略（fusion strategies），结合多种检测方法的优势来识别AI生成图像。

Result: 在CIFAKE数据集上实现了97.32%的准确率。

Conclusion: 该融合方法能有效区分AI生成图像与真实图像，有助于推进数据认证技术的发展，应对合成数据泛滥的问题。

Abstract: Recent advancements in synthetic data technology have opened a new era where images of remarkable quality are generated, blurring the lines between real-life images and those produced by Artificial Intelligence (AI). This evolution poses a significant challenge to ensuring the reliability and authenticity of data, underscoring the need for robust detection methods. In this paper, we present a robust approach aimed at addressing these pressing concerns. Our methodology revolves around leveraging fusion strategies, combining the strengths of multiple detection methods for identifying AI-generated images. Through extensive experimentation on the CIFAKE dataset, our model showcases remarkable performance, achieving an impressive accuracy rate of 97.32%. This accomplishment underscores the efficacy of our approach in accurately distinguishing between AI-generated images and real-life images, thus contributing to the advancement of data authentication techniques amidst the proliferation of synthetic data.

</details>


### [28] [CascadeOcc: Rethinking 3D Occupancy World Models with Cascaded VQ Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27644)
*Kyumin Hwang,Wonhyeok Choi,Jaeyeul Kim,Jihun Park,Daehee Park,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeOcc提出了一种基于级联VQ机制的占用世界模型，通过空间和时间的双层次架构实现从粗到细的4D占用预测与运动规划，在视觉方法中取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用世界模型过度依赖外部模态或大语言模型，未能充分利用占用表示本身固有的结构潜力。

Method: 1) 将级联向量量化(VQ)机制集成到自回归框架中；2) 遵循从粗到细原则的多尺度架构逐步细化细节；3) 引入TimeMixer捕捉多尺度时间依赖，建立时空双层次机制。

Result: 在4D占用预测和运动规划基准上，CascadeOcc在纯视觉方法中取得了最优性能。

Conclusion: 优化内在表示能力是依赖外部基础模型的有力替代方案。

Abstract: This letter proposes CascadeOcc, a novel occupancy world model that prioritizes intrinsic structural hierarchy over extrinsic auxiliary modalities for autonomous driving. Occupancy world models -- forecasting the future driving environment and planning the driving trajectory -- effectively bridge perception and planning, but current approaches often heavily rely on external modalities or large language models, failing to fully exploit the inherent structural potential of occupancy representations themselves. To enhance representational capacity for complex 3D scenes, we integrate a cascaded Vector Quantized (VQ) mechanism into an autoregressive framework. Following a coarse-to-fine principle, CascadeOcc progressively refines fine-grained details from global structures through a multi-scale architecture. Additionally, we incorporate a TimeMixer to capture multi-scale temporal dependencies, establishing a dual-hierarchy mechanism in both space and time. Experimental results on 4D occupancy forecasting and motion planning benchmarks demonstrate that CascadeOcc achieves superior performance among vision-centric approaches, validating that optimizing inherent representations is a powerful alternative to relying on external foundation models.

</details>


### [29] [VLM-Aware Meta-Optic Front-End Design for Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27646)
*Chanik Kang,Raphaël Pestourie,Haejun Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CODA提出了一种端到端联合优化框架，将连续密度超构光学前端与冻结的CLIP分类器直接对齐，优化交叉熵损失而非传统像差校正，在受限于超构光学的成像条件下将ImageNet-100的零样本准确率从53.75%提升至65.41%，并展现了跨模型、跨数据集的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成像系统依赖高质量光学元件，但在尺寸、成本受限场景下难以应用；紧凑型超构光学虽为替代方案，但其物理效率受限。现有的光学设计以图像级指标（分辨率、像差校正）为导向，未必有利于下游识别任务。作者旨在探索一种直接面向下游模型识别性能的光学设计新范式。

Method: 提出CODA（Co-Design of Optics and Algorithm）框架，采用可微成像模型与基于麦克斯韦仿真的伴随梯度更新，联合优化连续密度超构光学前端；固定CLIP模型权重，直接以交叉熵损失为优化目标，无需学习重建、ISP或图像保真度辅助损失。

Result: 在二维模拟成像ImageNet-100基准上，CLIP ViT-L/14零样本准确率从焦点集中基线的53.75%提升至65.41%；优化后的光学器件无需重新优化即可迁移至CLIP、SigLIP、DINOv2等多模型以及CIFAR-100、Food-101等数据集。

Conclusion: 在受限于超构光学的成像条件下，将光学设计与冻结视觉模型目标对齐比传统图像形成准则更有效地提升下游识别性能，为紧凑型光学系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Conventional machine-vision pipelines typically rely on high-quality optics that produce clean, human-interpretable images, and optical design has therefore been driven by image-level criteria such as resolution, aberration correction, and pixel fidelity. However, such optics are often impractical for size-, cost-, or form-factor-constrained applications, where compact meta-optics offer an attractive alternative but operate under strict physical efficiency limits. We propose CODA, a co-design framework that optimizes a continuous-density meta-optic front-end for frozen-model recognition using differentiable image formation and adjoint-gradient updates of Maxwell-based simulations. CODA directly optimizes the cross-entropy loss of a fixed zero-shot CLIP classifier without learned reconstruction, image signal processing, or image-fidelity auxiliary objectives. In a two-dimensional simulated imaging benchmark on ImageNet-100, CODA improves CLIP ViT-L/14 zero-shot accuracy from 53.75 $\pm$ 3.57$\%$ with a focal-concentration baseline to 65.41 $\pm$ 3.99$\%$. The optimized optics further transfer without re-optimization across CLIP, SigLIP, and DINOv2 on ImageNet-100, CIFAR-100, and Food-101. These results demonstrate that, under constrained meta-optic imaging, downstream recognition can be improved by aligning optical design with frozen vision-model objectives rather than conventional image-formation criteria.

</details>


### [30] [Temporal-Emerged Prompting for Segment Anything in Multiframe Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27655)
*Yinghui Xing,Donghao Chu,Shizhou Zhang,Di Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TEP-SAM框架，通过时间涌现提示（Temporal-Emerged Prompting）引导Segment Anything Model（SAM），结合全局运动模式与局部运动偏差，实现低信噪比红外序列中小目标的分割与定位。该方法在极低SNR和复杂动态背景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低信噪比红外序列中，小目标在单帧中与背景难以区分，现有方法（包括使用基础模型和帧间关联机制的先进方法）仍无法有效检测。观察到目标随时间逐渐从背景中涌现并变得可区分，因此希望显式利用这种时间涌现线索来引导分割模型。

Method: 提出TEP-SAM框架，包含：(1) 联合建模全局运动模式与局部运动偏差以定位潜在目标；(2) 利用运动差异增强目标区域特征；(3) 生成时间涌现线索作为SAM的提示，实现非交互式分割；(4) 将大规模语义预训练与任务特定时间建模相结合，适配SAM到多帧红外小目标检测任务。

Result: 大量实验证明该方法有效，尤其在极低SNR条件和复杂动态背景下表现突出。

Conclusion: TEP-SAM通过时间涌现提示机制，成功将SAM适配到具有挑战性的多帧红外小目标检测任务，在低信噪比和复杂动态背景下实现有效检测。

Abstract: Accurately localizing and segmenting small targets in low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) infrared sequences remains a challenging task. Since targets are often indistinguishable from the background in individual frames, existing methods, even when equipped with advanced foundation model and powerful inter-frame association mechanisms, still fail to detect them. Motivated by the observation that targets tend to emerge gradually from the background over time and become distinguishable, we propose Temporal-Emerged Prompting for Segment Anything Model (TEP-SAM), a principled framework designed to explicitly exploit such temporal-emerged cues to modulate and prompt SAM. TEP-SAM operates by jointly modeling global motion patterns and local motion deviations to locate potential targets. It further enhances target region features by leveraging motion discrepancy, thereby generating temporal-emerged cues for SAM and enabling non-interactive segmentation. By bridging large-scale semantic pretraining with task-specific temporal modeling, TEP-SAM effectively adapts SAM to the challenging multiframe infrared small target detection task. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, particularly under severely low-SNR conditions and in complex dynamic background.

</details>


### [31] [GeoFace: Consistent Multi-View Face Generation with Geometry-Constrained Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27659)
*Yeji Choi,Jinhyeok Choi,Jaewon Min,Minkyung Kwon,Jin Hyeon Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFace提出了一种几何约束的多视图扩散框架，通过联合生成RGB图像和3D人脸几何（使用FLAME网格的UV位置图），并引入几何引导的注意力对齐损失，实现了从单张输入图像生成一致的多视角人脸，在RenderMe-360和NeRSemble数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在逐视图级别实现了逼真合成，但缺乏在视图间强制执行共享3D结构的显式机制，导致跨视角几何不一致。

Method: 提出统一双流框架，同时生成多视图RGB图像和3D人脸几何；两流通过共享注意力层交互；引入几何引导的注意力对齐损失，用3D一致对应监督外观与几何token间的交叉注意力；几何表示为FLAME网格导出的规范UV位置图。

Result: 在RenderMe-360和NeRSemble数据集上，GeoFace在视觉质量和跨视图几何一致性方面始终优于现有方法，并能实现更高效的3D重建。

Conclusion: 通过将显式3D几何约束融入多视图扩散框架，有效解决了跨视图几何不一致问题，提升了生成质量和3D重建效率。

Abstract: We present GeoFace, a geometry-constrained multi-view diffusion framework for consistent face generation from a single input. % While recent multi-view diffusion models achieve photorealistic synthesis at the per-view level, they lack an explicit mechanism to enforce a shared 3D structure across views, often leading to inconsistent geometry across viewpoints. To address this, GeoFace proposes a unified dual-stream framework for joint generation of multi-view RGB images and 3D face geometry, where the appearance and geometry streams interact through shared attention layers. To encourage the two streams to mutually constrain each other, we introduce a geometry-guided attention alignment loss that supervises the cross-attention between appearance and geometry tokens with 3D-consistent correspondences, enabling the appearance stream to correctly reference pose-invariant geometric cues for robust alignment across viewpoints. Geometry is represented as a canonical UV position map, derived from a FLAME mesh fitted to multi-view observations, serving as a view-invariant shared constraint across all generated views. Experiments on RenderMe-360 and NeRSemble demonstrate that GeoFace consistently outperforms existing methods in both visual quality and cross-view geometric consistency, facilitating more efficient 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [32] [MVPruner: Dynamic Token Pruning for Accelerating Multi-view Vision-Language Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27660)
*Nan Yang,Zhanwen Liu,Linfeng Zhang,Shangyu Xie,Yang Wang,Wenzhuo Zhou,Xiangmo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVPruner，一种两阶段自适应token剪枝方法，用于提升多视图VLMs在自动驾驶中的效率。该方案依据视图信息多样性和指令文本动态分配剪枝预算并选择token，在DriveMM上实现87.3%的FLOPs降低、4.97倍预填充加速，同时保持98.5%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token剪枝方法采用固定剪枝率分配和静态重要性度量，忽视了多视图之间动态的跨视图重要性差异以及推理过程中信息需求的演化，导致多视图VLMs在自动驾驶场景中效率低下。

Method: 提出MVPruner两阶段自适应剪枝方法：第一阶段基于每个视图的信息多样性分配剪枝预算，并保留跨阶段一致性贡献的token；第二阶段以指令文本为指导分配预算并选择token，确保任务对齐。

Result: 在四个基准上取得优异效果，DriveMM搭载MVPruner实现FLOPs降低87.3%，预填充阶段加速4.97倍，在DriveLM基准上保持98.5%的准确率。

Conclusion: 多视图VLMs在深层编码了任务相关的视图先验且具有动态信息需求，所提MVPruner通过两阶段自适应剪枝有效对齐模型动态信息需求，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持了高精度。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) improve generalization and interpretability in autonomous driving but suffer from efficiency issues due to long visual token sequences, particularly in standard multi-view settings. Existing token pruning methods employ fixed pruning rate allocation and static importance metrics, ignoring dynamic inter-view importance differences and the evolving information importance during inference. Our analysis reveals that multi-view VLMs inherently encode task-related view priors in deeper layers and exhibit dynamic information requirements. Motivated by these findings, we propose MVPruner, a two-stage adaptive token pruning method that aligns pruning behavior with the model's dynamic information requirements. The first stage allocates pruning budgets based on the information diversity of each view, and retains tokens with consistent contribution across stages, ensuring semantic representational capacity. The second stage allocates budgets and selects tokens guided by instruction text to guarantee task alignment. Experimental results on four benchmarks demonstrate the superior performance of our method. For example, DriveMM equipped with MVPruner achieves 87.3% reduction in FLOPs, 4.97* speedup in prefilling phase while retaining 98.5% accuracy on DriveLM benchmark.

</details>


### [33] [Explainable AI for Biodiversity Monitoring and Ecological Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27667)
*Brinnae Bent,Holly R. Houliston,Jiayi Zhou,Günel Aghakishiyeva,David W. Johnston*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张将可解释人工智能（XAI）作为生态模型验证的标准组成部分，通过图像分类、目标检测和图像分割三个任务，结合海豹检测和鲸类解剖分割两个案例，展示了XAI如何帮助识别生物意义线索、揭示虚假阳性、发现边缘和遮挡效应，并指导数据收集与模型优化，从而提升AI生态证据的可靠性和可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多计算机视觉模型难以检查，无法判断预测是基于生态有意义的信号还是虚假相关、采样偏差等伪影，这可能会破坏保护决策。保护从业者越来越需要理解模型为什么准确，而不仅仅是否准确。

Method: 提出将XAI作为生态模型验证的标准组件，应用于三种常见生态计算机视觉任务（图像分类、目标检测和图像分割），并通过两个航拍影像案例（海豹检测和鲸类解剖分割）进行实证。

Result: 解释方法能够识别生物意义线索，揭示由背景和形状混淆导致的虚假阳性，发现边缘和遮挡效应，并指导数据收集、增强和重训练策略。

Conclusion: XAI能使模型行为更透明、科学可质询，有助于确保AI支持的生态证据更可靠、可理解、可操作，对生物多样性保护具有重要意义。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming biodiversity monitoring by enabling automated analysis of ecological imagery collected from camera traps, drones, satellites, underwater platforms, and other sensing systems. These tools can expand the scale and speed of conservation assessments, yet many computer vision models remain difficult to inspect, making it challenging to determine whether predictions are based on ecologically meaningful signals or on spurious correlations, sampling biases, and other artifacts that may undermine conservation decisions. We argue that explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) should become a standard component of ecological model validation because conservation practitioners increasingly depend on understanding not only whether a model is accurate, but why it is accurate. We provide practical guidance for applying XAI to three common ecological computer vision tasks: image classification, object detection, and image segmentation. To illustrate how XAI can support ecological model auditing, refinement, and deployment, we present two case studies using aerial imagery: harbor seal detection and cetacean anatomical segmentation. These examples demonstrate how explanation methods can identify biologically meaningful cues, reveal false positives driven by background and shape confounds, uncover edge and occlusion effects, and guide data collection, augmentation, and retraining strategies. More broadly, they show how explainability can help assess whether model reasoning aligns with ecological understanding. We conclude by identifying key challenges and opportunities. By making model behavior more transparent and scientifically interrogable, XAI can help ensure that AI-supported ecological evidence is more reliable, understandable, and actionable for biodiversity conservation.

</details>


### [34] [Multi-Modal Conditioned High-Resolution Transformer for Urban Electromagnetic Field Map Prediction Download PDF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27671)

Abstract: Predicting electromagnetic field (EMF) strength in urban environments is essential for cellular network planning but computationally expensive with physics-based simulators. Our architecture uses a High-Resolution Transformer (HRFormer) backbone with two complementary conditioning mechanisms: Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) injects scalar antenna parameters into all backbone stages, while cross-attention fuses 1-D radiation pattern tokens with spatial features at the deepest stage. We further introduce transmitter-relative spatial channels encoding distance, proximity, and bearing from the antenna, enabling coordinate-consistent test-time augmentation (TTA) that reduces test MAE by 6.3%. To address the prediction difficulty imbalance across EMF maps, we design a composite loss combining masked L1, multi-scale structural similarity (MS-SSIM), and a focal L1 term that upweights high-signal pixels, outperforming individual loss components in all metrics. Our best model achieves a test MAE of 0.0461, a 25.2% improvement over a plain UNet baseline and 31.8% over an HRFormer-only baseline.Do-

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段提出空间连续非配对神经薛定谔桥（SC-UNSB），将图像翻译建模为熵正则化的最优传输过程，构建合成中间域以缓解跨域分布偏移；第二阶段在知识蒸馏中提出双级特征对齐策略，逐步对齐浅层结构特征和深层语义表征，促进域不变知识从源模型向目标模型迁移。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法有效缓解了域偏移和类别模糊问题，提升了跨域检测性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain diagnosis remains a major challenge in cervical cell pathology due to pronounced domain shifts across institutions and the subtle visual differences among disease stages, which jointly impair model generalization. To address these issues, this paper proposes a two-stage framework for cross-domain cervical cell detection. In the first stage, we propose the Spatially-Continuous Unpaired Neural Schrödinger Bridge (SC-UNSB), which constructs a synthetic intermediate domain to mitigate cross-domain distribution shifts by modeling image translation as an entropy-regularized optimal transport process. In the second stage, we propose a dual-level feature alignment strategy within a knowledge distillation, which progressively aligns shallow structural features and deep semantic representations to facilitate the transfer of domain-invariant knowledge from the source to the target model. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively mitigates domain shift and category ambiguity, improving the cross-domain detection performance.

</details>
